import { lazy, Suspense } from "react";
import { Routes, Route, Navigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { ProtectedRoute } from "./components/layout/ProtectedRoute";

const Login = lazy(() => import("./pages/Login/Login"));
const Dashboard = lazy(() => import("./pages/Dashboard/Dashboard"));
const CharacterSheet = lazy(
  () => import("./pages/CharacterSheet/CharacterSheet")
);
const Campaigns = lazy(() => import("./pages/Campaigns/Campaigns"));
const Campaign = lazy(() => import("./pages/Campaign/Campaign"));
const CampaignMap = lazy(
  () => import("./pages/CampaignMap/CampaignMap")
);
const Shop = lazy(() => import("./pages/Shop/Shop"));
const ClassCatalog = lazy(
  () => import("./pages/ClassCatalog/ClassCatalog")
);

const DiceRollerPage = lazy(() =>
  import("./pages/DiceRoller/DiceRollerPage")
);

const AppShell = lazy(() =>
  import("./components/layout/AppShell").then((module) => ({
    default: module.AppShell,
  }))
);

function App() {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <div className="route-loading" role="status" aria-live="polite">
          Carregando tela...
        </div>
      }
    >
      <Routes>
        {/* Login */}
        <Route path="/entra" element={<Login />} />
        <Route path="/entrar" element={<Login />} />

        {/* Ãrea protegida */}
        <Route
          element={
            <ProtectedRoute>
              <AppShell />
            </ProtectedRoute>
          }
        >
          {/* Dashboard */}
          <Route path="/" element={<Dashboard />} />

          {/* Campanhas */}
          <Route path="/campanhas" element={<Campaigns />} />

          {/* CatÃ¡logo global de classes */}
          <Route
            path="/regras/classes"
            element={<ClassCatalog />}
          />

          {/* Compatibilidade: loja sem campanha */}
          <Route
            path="/loja"
            element={<Navigate to="/campanhas" replace />}
          />

          {/* Campanha */}
          <Route
            path="/campanha/:id"
            element={<Campaign />}
          />

          {/* Mapa da campanha */}
          <Route
            path="/campanha/:id/mapa"
            element={<CampaignMap />}
          />

          {/* Rolador de dados */}
          <Route
            path="/campanha/:id/dados"
            element={
              <Suspense
                fallback={
                  <p role="status">
                    Carregando rolador de dados...
                  </p>
                }
              >
                <DiceRollerPage />
              </Suspense>
            }
          />

          {/* Loja da campanha */}
          <Route
            path="/campanha/:id/loja"
            element={<Shop />}
          />

          {/* Carrinho da loja */}
          <Route
            path="/campanha/:id/loja/carrinho"
            element={<Shop />}
          />

          {/* Ficha do personagem */}
          <Route
            path="/personagem/:id"
            element={<CharacterSheet />}
          />
        </Route>
      </Routes>
    </Suspense>
  );
}

export default App;

========== ARQUIVO: C:\Users\Ellen\rpg-platform\src\components\character\CharacterCard.tsx ==========

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import type { Character } from "../../types/character";
import { calculateDerivedCharacterStats, type CharacterRulesContext } from "../../domain/characterRules";
import { StatBar } from "../ui/StatBar";
import "./CharacterCard.css";

export function CharacterCard({
  character,
  rulesContext,
}: {
  character: Character;
  rulesContext?: CharacterRulesContext;
}) {
  const stats = calculateDerivedCharacterStats(character, rulesContext);
  const hpAtual = Math.min(character.hp.atual, stats.hpMax);

  return (
    <Link to={"/personagem/" + character.id} className="character-card">
      <div className="character-card-portrait">
        {character.portraitUrl ? (
          <img
            src={character.portraitUrl}
            alt={character.nome}
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              height: "100%",
              objectFit: "cover",
              borderRadius: "inherit",
              display: "block",
            }}
          />
        ) : (
          character.nome.charAt(0)
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="character-card-body">
        <h3>{character.nome}</h3>

        <p className="character-card-meta">
          {character.raca} Â· {character.classe} Â· NÃ­vel {character.nivel} Â· {character.xp ?? 0} XP
        </p>

        {character.campanhaId === null && (
          <p className="character-card-meta">Sem campanha</p>
        )}

        <StatBar
          label="Vida"
          atual={hpAtual}
          max={stats.hpMax}
          tone="wine"
        />
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

========== ARQUIVO: C:\Users\Ellen\rpg-platform\src\components\character\ClassInfoBox.tsx ==========

import type { ClassInfo } from "./classInfoUtils";
import "./ClassInfoBox.css";

function sinal(valor: number) {
  return (valor > 0 ? "+" : "") + valor;
}

export function ClassInfoBox({
  info,
  mensagemVazia,
}: {
  info: ClassInfo | null;
  mensagemVazia: string;
}) {
  if (!info) {
    return <p className="class-info-empty">{mensagemVazia}</p>;
  }

  const temBonus =
    info.bonusAtributos.length > 0 || info.bonusHp !== 0 || info.bonusMp !== 0;

  return (
    <div className="class-info">
      <h4 className="class-info-name">{info.nome}</h4>

      {info.descricao ? (
        <p className="class-info-text">{info.descricao}</p>
      ) : (
        <p className="class-info-empty">
          Esta classe ainda nÃ£o tem descriÃ§Ã£o cadastrada.
        </p>
      )}

      {temBonus && (
        <div className="class-info-bonuses">
          {info.bonusAtributos.map((item) => (
            <span key={item.chave} className="class-info-chip">
              {sinal(item.valor)} {item.rotulo}
            </span>
          ))}

          {info.bonusHp !== 0 && (
            <span className="class-info-chip">{sinal(info.bonusHp)} Vida</span>
          )}

          {info.bonusMp !== 0 && (
            <span className="class-info-chip">{sinal(info.bonusMp)} Mana</span>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {info.habilidades.length > 0 && (
        <div className="class-info-abilities">
          <h5>Habilidades</h5>

          <ul>
            {info.habilidades.map((item) => (
              <li key={item.nome}>
                <strong>{item.nome}</strong>
                {item.nivel !== undefined && <small> Â· NÃ­vel {item.nivel}</small>}
                {item.descricao && <p>{item.descricao}</p>}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { montarInfoClasse } from "./classInfoUtils";
import type { ClassDefinition } from "../../data/personaRules";

const classDefinition: ClassDefinition = {
  id: "guerreiro",
  nome: "Guerreiro",
  aliases: [],
  atributoBonus: { forca: 2, destreza: 0, constituicao: 0, inteligencia: 0, carisma: 0 },
  hpBonus: 10,
  mpBonus: 0,
  usaMagia: false,
  pericias: [],
  habilidades: [],
  magias: [],
};

describe("montarInfoClasse", () => {
  it("mostra o bÃ´nus efetivo da campanha acima do catÃ¡logo e habilidades com nÃ­vel", () => {
    const info = montarInfoClasse(
      "Guerreiro",
      "guerreiro",
      [classDefinition],
      [{
        id: "ability-1",
        campanha_id: "campaign-1",
        classe_id: "guerreiro",
        tipo: "HABILIDADE",
        nome: "Golpe tÃ¡tico",
        descricao: "Uma aÃ§Ã£o especial.",
        atributo: null,
        nivel: 4,
        bonus_atributos: {},
        bonus_hp: 0,
        bonus_mp: 0,
        created_by: "master-1",
      }],
      {
        Guerreiro: {
          atributoBonus: { forca: 0, destreza: 0, constituicao: 0, inteligencia: 2, carisma: 0 },
          hpBonus: 4,
          mpBonus: 3,
        },
      },
    );

    expect(info?.bonusAtributos).toEqual([{ chave: "inteligencia", rotulo: "InteligÃªncia", valor: 2 }]);
    expect(info?.bonusHp).toBe(4);
    expect(info?.bonusMp).toBe(3);
    expect(info?.habilidades).toContainEqual({
      nome: "Golpe tÃ¡tico",
      descricao: "Uma aÃ§Ã£o especial.",
      nivel: 4,
    });
  });
});



========== ARQUIVO: C:\Users\Ellen\rpg-platform\src\components\character\classInfoUtils.ts ==========

import type { Attributes } from "../../types/character";
import type { CampaignClassContent } from "../../services/api";
import {
  ATTRIBUTE_CONFIG,
  type CampaignClassBonuses,
  type ClassDefinition,
} from "../../data/personaRules";

export interface ClassInfo {
  nome: string;
  descricao: string;
  bonusAtributos: { chave: string; rotulo: string; valor: number }[];
  bonusHp: number;
  bonusMp: number;
  habilidades: { nome: string; descricao: string; nivel?: number }[];
}

type BonusAtributos = Partial<Record<keyof Attributes, number>>;
type HabilidadeBruta = { nome?: unknown; descricao?: unknown; nivel?: unknown };

function texto(valor: unknown): string {
  return typeof valor === "string" ? valor.trim() : "";
}

function normalizarChave(valor: string): string {
  return valor.normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
}

function resolverBonusCampanha(
  classeNome: string,
  classeId: string | undefined,
  bonuses: CampaignClassBonuses,
) {
  const entrada = Object.entries(bonuses).find(([key]) =>
    normalizarChave(key) === normalizarChave(classeNome) ||
    normalizarChave(key) === normalizarChave(classeId ?? ""),
  );
  return entrada?.[1];
}

/**
 * Apresenta a regra efetiva da classe, seguindo a mesma precedÃªncia do cÃ¡lculo:
 * override da campanha > classe criada na campanha > catÃ¡logo global/local.
 */
export function montarInfoClasse(
  nome: string | undefined,
  classeId: string | undefined,
  catalogo: ClassDefinition[],
  conteudos: CampaignClassContent[],
  bonusCampanha: CampaignClassBonuses = {},
): ClassInfo | null {
  if (!nome) return null;

  const chaveNome = normalizarChave(nome);
  const definicao = catalogo.find(
    (item) =>
      (classeId && item.id === classeId) ||
      normalizarChave(item.nome) === chaveNome ||
      item.aliases?.some((alias) => normalizarChave(alias) === chaveNome),
  );

  const idClasse = definicao?.id ?? classeId;
  const conteudoClasse = conteudos.find(
    (item) =>
      item.tipo === "CLASSE" &&
      ((idClasse && item.classe_id === idClasse) || normalizarChave(item.nome) === chaveNome),
  );
  const override = resolverBonusCampanha(nome, idClasse, bonusCampanha);

  if (!definicao && !conteudoClasse && !override) return null;

  const descricao =
    texto(conteudoClasse?.descricao) ||
    texto((definicao as (ClassDefinition & { descricao?: unknown }) | undefined)?.descricao);

  const bonusBrutos = (override?.atributoBonus ??
    conteudoClasse?.bonus_atributos ??
    definicao?.atributoBonus ??
    {}) as BonusAtributos;

  const bonusAtributos = ATTRIBUTE_CONFIG.map(({ key, label }) => ({
    chave: String(key),
    rotulo: label,
    valor: Number(bonusBrutos[key as keyof Attributes] ?? 0) || 0,
  })).filter((item) => item.valor !== 0);

  const habilidadesDefinicao = (
    (definicao?.habilidades ?? []) as unknown as HabilidadeBruta[]
  ).map((item) => ({
    nome: texto(item?.nome),
    descricao: texto(item?.descricao),
    nivel: typeof item?.nivel === "number" ? item.nivel : undefined,
  }));

  const habilidadesCampanha = conteudos
    .filter((item) => item.tipo === "HABILIDADE" && idClasse && item.classe_id === idClasse)
    .map((item) => ({
      nome: texto(item.nome),
      descricao: texto(item.descricao),
      nivel: typeof item.nivel === "number" ? item.nivel : undefined,
    }));

  const habilidades = [...habilidadesDefinicao, ...habilidadesCampanha].filter(
    (item, index, lista) =>
      item.nome && lista.findIndex((outro) => outro.nome === item.nome) === index,
  );

  return {
    nome: definicao?.nome ?? conteudoClasse?.nome ?? nome,
    descricao,
    bonusAtributos,
    bonusHp: Number(override?.hpBonus ?? conteudoClasse?.bonus_hp ?? definicao?.hpBonus ?? 0) || 0,
    bonusMp: Number(override?.mpBonus ?? conteudoClasse?.bonus_mp ?? definicao?.mpBonus ?? 0) || 0,
    habilidades,
  };
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  alterarItemEquipado,
  getInventory,
  usarItemPersonagem,
  type ItemUseReceipt,
} from "../../services/api";
import "./InventoryItems.css";

type InventoryItemData = {
  id: string;
  bonus_atributos?: Record<string, number> | null;
  nome: string;
  descricao: string | null;
  tipo: string | null;
  raridade: string | null;
  efeito: string | null;
  imagem_url: string | null;
  efeito_mecanico?: { tipo: string; recurso?: "hp" | "mp"; valor?: number } | null;
  cargas_maximas?: number | null;
};

type InventoryRow = {
  personagem_id: string;
  item_id: string;
  quantidade: number;
  cargas_atuais?: number | null;
  equipado?: boolean;
  itens: InventoryItemData | InventoryItemData[] | null;
};

type PersistedInventoryItem = {
  itemId: string;
  quantidade: number;
  cargasAtuais: number | null;
  equipado: boolean;
  item: InventoryItemData | null;
};

type InventoryItemsProps = {
  characterId: string;
  campaignId?: string | null;
  onEquipmentChanged?: () => void;
  onItemUsed?: (receipt: ItemUseReceipt) => void | Promise<void>;
};

function normalizeInventoryRows(
  rows: InventoryRow[],
): PersistedInventoryItem[] {
  return rows.map((row) => {
    const quantidade = Number(row.quantidade) || 0;
    const item = Array.isArray(row.itens) ? row.itens[0] ?? null : row.itens;
    return {
      itemId: row.item_id,
      quantidade,
      cargasAtuais: row.cargas_atuais ?? (item?.cargas_maximas ? item.cargas_maximas * quantidade : null),
      equipado: row.equipado === true,
      item,
    };
  });
}

export default function InventoryItems({
  characterId,
  campaignId,
  onEquipmentChanged,
  onItemUsed,
}: InventoryItemsProps) {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<PersistedInventoryItem[]>([]);
  const [loadResult, setLoadResult] = useState<{
    requestKey: string;
    error: string;
  } | null>(null);
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState("");
  const requestKey = `${characterId}:${attempt}`;
  const loading = loadResult?.requestKey !== requestKey;
  const loadError = loadResult?.requestKey === requestKey ? loadResult.error : "";
  const error = actionError || loadError;
  const [alterandoItem, setAlterandoItem] = useState<string | null>(
    null,
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    getInventory(characterId)
      .then((data) => {
        if (active) {
          setItems(normalizeInventoryRows(data as InventoryRow[]));
          setLoadResult({ requestKey, error: "" });
        }
      })
      .catch((err: unknown) => {
        console.error("Erro ao carregar inventÃ¡rio:", err);

        if (active) {
          setItems([]);
          setLoadResult({
            requestKey,
            error:
              err instanceof Error
                ? err.message
                : "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel carregar o inventÃ¡rio.",
          });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [characterId, requestKey]);

  async function usarItem(item: PersistedInventoryItem) {
    if (!item.item || (!item.item.efeito_mecanico || item.item.efeito_mecanico.tipo === "NENHUM") && item.item.tipo !== "CONSUMIVEL" && !item.item.cargas_maximas) return;
    setAlterandoItem(item.itemId);
    setActionError("");
    try {
      const receipt = await usarItemPersonagem(characterId, item.itemId);
      setItems((current) => current.map((entry) => entry.itemId === item.itemId
        ? { ...entry, quantidade: receipt.quantidade, cargasAtuais: item.item?.cargas_maximas ? Math.max(0, (item.cargasAtuais ?? 0) - 1) : entry.cargasAtuais }
        : entry).filter((entry) => entry.quantidade > 0));
      await onItemUsed?.(receipt);
      setAttempt((current) => current + 1);
    } catch (error) {
      setActionError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel usar o item.");
    } finally {
      setAlterandoItem(null);
    }
  }

  async function alternarEquipamento(
    item: PersistedInventoryItem,
  ) {
    if (!item.itemId) {
      return;
    }

    setAlterandoItem(item.itemId);
    setActionError("");

    const novoEstado = !item.equipado;

    try {
      await alterarItemEquipado(
        characterId,
        item.itemId,
        novoEstado,
      );

      setItems((atual) =>
        atual.map((itemAtual) =>
          itemAtual.itemId === item.itemId
            ? {
                ...itemAtual,
                equipado: novoEstado,
              }
            : itemAtual,
        ),
      );
      onEquipmentChanged?.();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      console.error(
        "Erro ao alterar equipamento:",
        err,
      );

      setActionError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel alterar o equipamento.",
      );
    } finally {
      setAlterandoItem(null);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <p
        className="status-message"
        role="status"
      >
        Carregando inventÃ¡rio...
      </p>
    );
  }

  if (error && items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div
        className="status-message status-message--error"
        role="alert"
      >
        <p>{error}</p>

        <button
          type="button"
          className="btn-ghost inventory-retry"
          onClick={() => {
            setActionError("");
            setAttempt((current) => current + 1);
          }}
        >
          Tentar novamente
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="inventory-empty">
        <h2>InventÃ¡rio vazio</h2>

        <p>
          O inventÃ¡rio persistido deste personagem estÃ¡ vazio.
        </p>

        {campaignId && (
          <Link
            to={`/campanha/${campaignId}/loja`}
            className="btn-primary"
          >
            Adicionar itens pela loja
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="inventory-content">
      <div className="inventory-actions">
        <h2 className="inventory-section-title">
          Itens do personagem
        </h2>

        {campaignId && (
          <Link
            to={`/campanha/${campaignId}/loja`}
            className="btn-ghost inventory-shop-link"
          >
            Adicionar itens pela loja
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div
          className="status-message status-message--error"
          role="alert"
        >
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <div
        className="inventory-grid"
        role="region"
        aria-label="InventÃ¡rio persistido do personagem"
      >
        {items.map((inventoryItem) => {
          const estaEquipado =
            inventoryItem.equipado === true;

          const alterando =
            alterandoItem === inventoryItem.itemId;

          return (
            <article
              key={inventoryItem.itemId}
              className={
                estaEquipado
                  ? "inventory-item inventory-item--equipped"
                  : "inventory-item"
              }
            >
              <div className="inventory-item-head">
                {inventoryItem.item?.imagem_url && (
                  <img
                    className="inventory-item-image"
                    src={inventoryItem.item.imagem_url}
                    alt=""
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    onError={(event) => {
                      event.currentTarget.hidden = true;
                    }}
                  />
                )}

                <h2 className="inventory-item-title">
                  {inventoryItem.item
                    ? inventoryItem.item.nome
                    : "Item desconhecido"}
                </h2>

                <span className="inventory-item-quantity">
                  x{inventoryItem.quantidade}
                </span>
              </div>

              {(inventoryItem.item?.tipo ||
                inventoryItem.item?.raridade ||
                estaEquipado) && (
                <div className="inventory-item-meta">
                  {(inventoryItem.item?.tipo ||
                    inventoryItem.item?.raridade) && (
                    <span className="inventory-item-kind">
                      {[
                        inventoryItem.item?.tipo,
                        inventoryItem.item?.raridade,
                      ]
                        .filter(Boolean)
                        .join(" Â· ")}
                    </span>
                  )}

              {estaEquipado && (
                    <span className="inventory-item-equipped">
                      Equipado
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {inventoryItem.item?.cargas_maximas && (
                    <span className="inventory-item-equipped">
                      Cargas {inventoryItem.cargasAtuais ?? 0}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}

              {inventoryItem.item?.descricao && (
                <p className="inventory-item-description">
                  {inventoryItem.item.descricao}
                </p>
              )}

              {inventoryItem.item?.efeito && (
                <p className="inventory-item-effect">
                  âœ¦ {inventoryItem.item.efeito}
                </p>
              )}

              <div className="inventory-item-actions">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className={
                    estaEquipado
                      ? "btn-ghost inventory-equip-button inventory-equip-button--equipped"
                      : "btn-primary inventory-equip-button"
                  }
                  disabled={alterando}
                  onClick={() => void alternarEquipamento(inventoryItem)}
                >
                  {alterando
                    ? "Salvando..."
                    : estaEquipado
                      ? "Desequipar"
                      : "Equipar"}
                </button>
                {(inventoryItem.item?.tipo === "CONSUMIVEL" || (inventoryItem.item?.cargas_maximas ?? 0) > 0) && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="btn-ghost inventory-use-button"
                    disabled={alterando || inventoryItem.quantidade <= 0 || (inventoryItem.cargasAtuais !== null && inventoryItem.cargasAtuais <= 0)}
                    onClick={() => void usarItem(inventoryItem)}
                  >
                    {alterando ? "Usando..." : "Usar"}
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </article>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



========== ARQUIVO: C:\Users\Ellen\rpg-platform\src\components\layout\AppShell.css ==========

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { NavLink, Outlet, useMatch } from "react-router-dom";
import { Crest } from "../ui/Crest";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/useAuth";
import { listarMinhasCampanhas } from "../../services/api";
import "./AppShell.css";
import "./AppShellCampaign.css";

export function AppShell() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();

  // Dentro das rotas de campanha, o menu mostra os destinos daquela campanha.
  const campanhaAtual = useMatch("/campanha/:id/*");
  const campanhaId = campanhaAtual?.params.id;
  const personagemFichaAtual = useMatch("/personagem/:id");
  const personagemAtual = Boolean(personagemFichaAtual);
  const [nomeCampanha, setNomeCampanha] = useState("");
  const [menuAberto, setMenuAberto] = useState(false);
  const [erroLogout, setErroLogout] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!campanhaId) return;

    let ativo = true;

    listarMinhasCampanhas()
      .then((campanhas) => {
        if (ativo) {
          setNomeCampanha(
            campanhas.find((item) => item.id === campanhaId)?.nome ?? "",
          );
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (ativo) setNomeCampanha("");
      });

    return () => {
      ativo = false;
    };
  }, [campanhaId]);

  return (
    <div className="app-shell">
      <aside className={menuAberto ? "app-sidebar app-sidebar--open" : "app-sidebar"}>
        <div className="app-sidebar-header">
          <div className="app-sidebar-brand">
            <Crest size={30} />
            <span>Elementum</span>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn-ghost app-sidebar-menu-toggle"
            aria-controls="app-sidebar-navigation"
            aria-expanded={menuAberto}
            onClick={() => setMenuAberto((aberto) => !aberto)}
          >
            {menuAberto ? "Fechar menu" : "Menu"}
            <span aria-hidden="true">{menuAberto ? "âˆ’" : "+"}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <nav id="app-sidebar-navigation" className="app-sidebar-nav" aria-label="NavegaÃ§Ã£o principal">
          <NavLink
            to="/"
            end
            className={({ isActive }) => isActive || personagemAtual ? "active" : undefined}
            onClick={() => setMenuAberto(false)}
          >
            Meus personagens
          </NavLink>

          <NavLink to="/campanhas" end onClick={() => setMenuAberto(false)}>
            Campanhas
          </NavLink>

          <NavLink to="/regras/classes" onClick={() => setMenuAberto(false)}>
            Classes e habilidades
          </NavLink>

          {campanhaId && (
            <>
              <span className="app-sidebar-section">
                {nomeCampanha || "Campanha atual"}
              </span>

              <NavLink to={"/campanha/" + campanhaId} end onClick={() => setMenuAberto(false)}>
                VisÃ£o geral
              </NavLink>
              <NavLink to={"/campanha/" + campanhaId + "/mapa"} onClick={() => setMenuAberto(false)}>
                Mapa
              </NavLink>
              <NavLink to={"/campanha/" + campanhaId + "/loja"} onClick={() => setMenuAberto(false)}>
                Loja
              </NavLink>
              <NavLink to={"/campanha/" + campanhaId + "/dados"} onClick={() => setMenuAberto(false)}>
                Dados
              </NavLink>
            </>
          )}
        </nav>

        <div className="app-sidebar-footer">
          <div
            className="app-sidebar-account"
            aria-label={`Conta conectada: ${user?.nome || "Aventureiro"}`}
          >
            <span className="app-sidebar-account-mark" aria-hidden="true">
              {user?.nome?.trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase() || "A"}
            </span>

            <span className="app-sidebar-account-details">
              <span className="app-sidebar-account-label">Conta conectada</span>
              <span className="app-sidebar-user" title={user?.nome}>
                {user?.nome || "Aventureiro"}
              </span>
            </span>
          </div>

          {erroLogout && <p className="app-sidebar-error" role="alert">{erroLogout}</p>}
          <button
            className="btn-ghost app-sidebar-logout"
            aria-label="Sair da conta"
            onClick={() => {
              setErroLogout("");
              void logout().catch((error: unknown) => {
                setErroLogout(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel sair da conta.");
              });
            }}
          >
            Sair
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <main className="app-content">
        <Outlet />
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

========== ARQUIVO: C:\Users\Ellen\rpg-platform\src\components\layout\ProtectedRoute.tsx ==========

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Navigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/useAuth";

export function ProtectedRoute({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) return null;
  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/entrar" replace />;

  return <>{children}</>;
}

========== ARQUIVO: C:\Users\Ellen\rpg-platform\src\components\ui\OptionField.tsx ==========

import { useState } from "react";

const OUTRA = "__outra__";

type OptionFieldProps = {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  options: string[];
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  wide?: boolean;
};

export function OptionField({
  label,
  value,
  options,
  onChange,
  wide,
}: OptionFieldProps) {
  const valorPersonalizado = value !== "" && !options.includes(value);
  const [outra, setOutra] = useState(valorPersonalizado);
  const mostrarCampo = outra || valorPersonalizado;

  return (
    <label className={wide ? "field field-wide" : "field"}>
      <span>{label}</span>

      <select
        value={mostrarCampo ? OUTRA : value}
        onChange={(event) => {
          if (event.target.value === OUTRA) {
            setOutra(true);
            onChange("");
          } else {
            setOutra(false);
            onChange(event.target.value);
          }
        }}
      >
        <option value="">Selecione...</option>

        {options.map((opcao) => (
          <option key={opcao} value={opcao}>
            {opcao}
          </option>
        ))}

        <option value={OUTRA}>Outro...</option>
      </select>

      {mostrarCampo && (
        <input
          value={value}
          onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
          placeholder="Digite aqui..."
        />
      )}
    </label>
  );
}

========== ARQUIVO: C:\Users\Ellen\rpg-platform\src\components\ui\StatBar.tsx ==========

import "./StatBar.css";

interface StatBarProps {
  label: string;
  atual: number;
  max: number;
  tone?: "wine" | "forest";
}

export function StatBar({ label, atual, max, tone = "wine" }: StatBarProps) {
  const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (atual / max) * 100));
  return (
    <div className="stat-bar">
      <div className="stat-bar-head">
        <span>{label}</span>
        <span>
          {atual} / {max}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="stat-bar-track">
        <div
          className={`stat-bar-fill stat-bar-fill--${tone}`}
          style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { createContext } from "react";
import type { User } from "../types/character";

export interface AuthContextValue {
  user: User | null;
  loading: boolean;
  login: (usuario: string, senha: string) => Promise<void>;
  register: (usuario: string, email: string, senha: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
}

export const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | undefined>(undefined);



========== ARQUIVO: C:\Users\Ellen\rpg-platform\src\context\AuthContext.tsx ==========

import { useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import type { User } from "../types/character";
import { AuthContext } from "./auth-context";

function mapSupabaseUser(
  supabaseUser: {
    id: string;
    email?: string | null;
    user_metadata?: {
      nome?: string;
      username?: string;
    };
  } | null,
): User | null {
  if (!supabaseUser) {
    return null;
  }

  const nome =
    supabaseUser.user_metadata?.nome ||
    supabaseUser.user_metadata?.username ||
    supabaseUser.email?.split("@")[0] ||
    "Aventureiro";

  return {
    id: supabaseUser.id,
    nome,
    email: supabaseUser.email ?? undefined,
  };
}

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;
    let unsubscribe: (() => void) | undefined;

    async function loadSession() {
      try {
        const { supabase } = await import("../services/supabase");
        if (!mounted) return;

        const {
          data: { subscription },
        } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
          if (mounted) {
            setUser(mapSupabaseUser(session?.user ?? null));
            setLoading(false);
          }
        });
        unsubscribe = () => subscription.unsubscribe();

        const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

        if (error) {
          console.error("Erro ao recuperar sessÃ£o:", error);
        }

        if (mounted) {
          setUser(mapSupabaseUser(data.session?.user ?? null));
          setLoading(false);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Erro ao recuperar sessÃ£o:", error);
        if (mounted) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    void loadSession();

    return () => {
      mounted = false;
      unsubscribe?.();
    };
  }, []);

  async function login(usuario: string, senha: string) {
    const { login: loginRequest } = await import("../services/api");
    setUser(await loginRequest(usuario, senha));
  }

  async function register(usuario: string, email: string, senha: string) {
    const { registerUser: registerRequest } = await import("../services/api");
    const newUser = await registerRequest(usuario, email, senha);
    const { supabase } = await import("../services/supabase");
    const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

    setUser(data.session ? newUser : null);
  }

  async function logout() {
    const { supabase } = await import("../services/supabase");
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signOut();

    if (error) {
      throw new Error(error.message || "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel sair do reino.");
    }

    setUser(null);
  }

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, loading, login, register, logout }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}



========== ARQUIVO: C:\Users\Ellen\rpg-platform\src\context\useAuth.ts ==========

import { useContext } from "react";
import { AuthContext } from "./auth-context";

export function useAuth() {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useAuth deve ser usado dentro de <AuthProvider>");
  }

  return context;
}

  Paladino: 10,
  Patrulheiro: 10,
  Bardo: 8,
  Bruxo: 8,
  ClÃ©rigo: 8,
  Druida: 8,
  Ladino: 8,
  Monge: 8,
  Feiticeiro: 6,
  Mago: 6,
};

export function modificador(valor: number): number {
  return Math.floor((valor - 10) / 2);
}

export function formatarModificador(valor: number): string {
  return (valor >= 0 ? "+" : "") + valor;
}

export function bonusProficiencia(nivel: number): number {
  return Math.ceil(nivel / 4) + 1;
}

export const LIMITE_RECURSO = 200;

/** Vida mÃ¡xima: dado de vida cheio no nÃ­vel 1, mÃ©dia do dado nos nÃ­veis seguintes. */
export function calcularVidaMaxima(
  classe: string,
  nivel: number,
  constituicao: number,
): number {
  const dado = DADO_DE_VIDA[classe] ?? 8;
  const mod = modificador(constituicao);
  const porNivel = Math.max(1, Math.floor(dado / 2) + 1 + mod);

  return Math.min(
    LIMITE_RECURSO,
    Math.max(1, dado + mod + (nivel - 1) * porNivel),
  );
}

/** Mesma regra que o projeto jÃ¡ usava para a mana. */
export function calcularManaMaxima(inteligencia: number): number {
  return Math.min(LIMITE_RECURSO, Math.max(0, 8 + inteligencia));
}

export function carteiraInicial(ouro: number): Wallet {
  return { pc: 0, pp: 0, pe: 0, po: Math.max(0, ouro), pl: 0 };
}

const classeId = (nome: string) =>
  nome.normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "");

export const DEFAULT_CLASS_CATALOG: ClassDefinition[] = Object.entries(
  CLASS_BONUSES,
).map(([nome, bonus]) => ({
  id: classeId(nome),
  nome,
  aliases: [],
  usaMagia: false,
  atributoBonus: {
    forca: bonus.atributoBonus?.forca ?? 0,
    destreza: bonus.atributoBonus?.destreza ?? 0,
    constituicao: bonus.atributoBonus?.constituicao ?? 0,
    inteligencia: bonus.atributoBonus?.inteligencia ?? 0,
    carisma: bonus.atributoBonus?.carisma ?? 0,
  },
  hpBonus: bonus.hpBonus ?? 0,
  mpBonus: bonus.mpBonus ?? 0,
  pericias: [],
  habilidades: [],
  magias: [],
}));

function textoSeguro(valor: unknown): string {
  return typeof valor === "string" ? valor.trim().slice(0, 500) : "";
}

function numeroSeguro(valor: unknown, fallback = 0): number {
  return typeof valor === "number" && Number.isFinite(valor)
    ? Math.trunc(valor)
    : fallback;
}

const ATRIBUTOS_BASE: BaseAttributeKey[] = [
  "forca",
  "destreza",
  "constituicao",
  "inteligencia",
  "carisma",
];

export function normalizarCatalogoClasses(valor: unknown): ClassDefinition[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(valor)) return DEFAULT_CLASS_CATALOG;

  return valor.flatMap((item): ClassDefinition[] => {
    if (typeof item !== "object" || item === null) return [];
    const entrada = item as Record<string, unknown>;
    const id = textoSeguro(entrada.id);
    const nome = textoSeguro(entrada.nome);
    if (!id || !nome) return [];
    const bonusEntrada =
      typeof entrada.atributoBonus === "object" && entrada.atributoBonus !== null
        ? (entrada.atributoBonus as Record<string, unknown>)
        : {};
    const atributoBonus = Object.fromEntries(
      ATRIBUTOS_BASE.map((atributo) => [
        atributo,
        numeroSeguro(bonusEntrada[atributo]),
      ]),
    ) as Record<BaseAttributeKey, number>;
    const pericias: ClassSkillDefinition[] = Array.isArray(entrada.pericias)
      ? entrada.pericias.flatMap((itemPericia): ClassSkillDefinition[] => {
          if (typeof itemPericia !== "object" || itemPericia === null) return [];
          const pericia = itemPericia as Record<string, unknown>;
          const periciaId = textoSeguro(pericia.id);
          const nomePericia = textoSeguro(pericia.nome);
          const atributo = pericia.atributo;
          if (
            !periciaId ||
            !nomePericia ||
            typeof atributo !== "string" ||
            !ATRIBUTOS_BASE.includes(atributo as BaseAttributeKey)
          ) return [];
          return [{
            id: periciaId,
            nome: nomePericia,
            atributo: atributo as BaseAttributeKey,
            descricao: textoSeguro(pericia.descricao),
          }];
        })
      : [];
    const habilidades: ClassAbilityDefinition[] = Array.isArray(entrada.habilidades)
      ? entrada.habilidades.flatMap((itemHabilidade): ClassAbilityDefinition[] => {
          if (typeof itemHabilidade !== "object" || itemHabilidade === null) return [];
          const habilidade = itemHabilidade as Record<string, unknown>;
          const habilidadeId = textoSeguro(habilidade.id);
          const nomeHabilidade = textoSeguro(habilidade.nome);
          if (!habilidadeId || !nomeHabilidade) return [];
          return [{
            id: habilidadeId,
            nome: nomeHabilidade,
            descricao: textoSeguro(habilidade.descricao),
            nivel: Math.max(1, Math.min(20, numeroSeguro(habilidade.nivel, 1))),
          }];
        })
      : [];
    const magias: ClassSpellDefinition[] = Array.isArray(entrada.magias)
      ? entrada.magias.flatMap((itemMagia): ClassSpellDefinition[] => {
          if (typeof itemMagia !== "object" || itemMagia === null) return [];
          const magia = itemMagia as Record<string, unknown>;
          const magiaId = textoSeguro(magia.id);
          const nomeMagia = textoSeguro(magia.nome);
          if (!magiaId || !nomeMagia) return [];
          return [{
            id: magiaId,
            nome: nomeMagia,
            descricao: textoSeguro(magia.descricao),
            custo: Math.max(0, numeroSeguro(magia.custo)),
            nivel: Math.max(1, Math.min(20, numeroSeguro(magia.nivel, 1))),
          }];
        })
      : [];

    return [{
      id,
      nome,
      aliases: Array.isArray(entrada.aliases)
        ? entrada.aliases.map(textoSeguro).filter(Boolean)
        : [],
      usaMagia: entrada.usaMagia === true,
      atributoBonus,
      hpBonus: numeroSeguro(entrada.hpBonus),
      mpBonus: numeroSeguro(entrada.mpBonus),
      pericias,
      habilidades,
      magias,
    }];
  });
}

export function normalizarBonusClassesCampanha(
  valores?: unknown,
  incluirPadroes = true,
): CampaignClassBonuses {
  const entrada =
    typeof valores === "object" && valores !== null
      ? (valores as Record<string, unknown>)
      : {};
  const resultado: CampaignClassBonuses = {};

  for (const [classe, definicao] of incluirPadroes
    ? Object.entries(CLASS_BONUSES)
    : []) {
    const personalizado =
      typeof entrada[classe] === "object" && entrada[classe] !== null
        ? (entrada[classe] as Record<string, unknown>)
        : {};
    const atributosPersonalizados =
      typeof personalizado.atributoBonus === "object" &&
      personalizado.atributoBonus !== null
        ? (personalizado.atributoBonus as Record<string, unknown>)
        : {};
    const atributoBonus: Record<BaseAttributeKey, number> = {
      forca: definicao.atributoBonus?.forca ?? 0,
      destreza: definicao.atributoBonus?.destreza ?? 0,
      constituicao: definicao.atributoBonus?.constituicao ?? 0,
      inteligencia: definicao.atributoBonus?.inteligencia ?? 0,
      carisma: definicao.atributoBonus?.carisma ?? 0,
    };

    for (const atributo of Object.keys(atributoBonus) as BaseAttributeKey[]) {
      const valor = atributosPersonalizados[atributo];
      if (typeof valor === "number" && Number.isFinite(valor)) {
        atributoBonus[atributo] = Math.trunc(valor);
      }
    }

    resultado[classe] = {
      atributoBonus,
      hpBonus:
        typeof personalizado.hpBonus === "number" &&
        Number.isFinite(personalizado.hpBonus)
          ? Math.trunc(personalizado.hpBonus)
          : definicao.hpBonus ?? 0,
      mpBonus:
        typeof personalizado.mpBonus === "number" &&
        Number.isFinite(personalizado.mpBonus)
          ? Math.trunc(personalizado.mpBonus)
          : definicao.mpBonus ?? 0,
    };
  }

  for (const [classe, entradaClasse] of Object.entries(entrada)) {
    if (classe in resultado || typeof entradaClasse !== "object" || entradaClasse === null) {
      continue;
    }
    const personalizado = entradaClasse as Record<string, unknown>;
    const atributos =
      typeof personalizado.atributoBonus === "object" && personalizado.atributoBonus !== null
        ? (personalizado.atributoBonus as Record<string, unknown>)
        : {};
    const atributoBonus = Object.fromEntries(
      ATRIBUTOS_BASE.map((atributo) => [
        atributo,
        numeroSeguro(atributos[atributo]),
      ]),
    ) as Record<BaseAttributeKey, number>;
    resultado[classe] = {
      atributoBonus,
      hpBonus: numeroSeguro(personalizado.hpBonus),
      mpBonus: numeroSeguro(personalizado.mpBonus),
    };
  }

  return resultado;
}

    carteira: { pc: 2, pp: 3, pe: 1, po: 15, pl: 0 },
    notas: "",
    campaignSpecificLegacyField: { custom: true },
  };
}

describe("character data contract", () => {
  it("migrates legacy shape to V1 without persisted max values", () => {
    const parsed = parseCharacterData(legacyCharacter());

    expect(parsed).toMatchObject({
      schemaVersion: 1,
      nome: "Nara",
      hp: { atual: 17, temp: 4 },
      mp: { atual: 5 },
      attributes,
      carteira: { pc: 2, pp: 3, pe: 1, po: 15, pl: 0 },
      campaignSpecificLegacyField: { custom: true },
    });
    expect(parsed?.hp).not.toHaveProperty("max");
    expect(parsed?.mp).not.toHaveProperty("max");
  });

  it("serializes only the V1 data contract and preserves temporary resources", () => {
    const parsed = parseCharacterData(legacyCharacter());
    expect(parsed).not.toBeNull();
    const character = {
      ...parsed!,
      id: "character-1",
      campanhaId: "campaign-1",
      dataVersion: 8,
      hp: { ...parsed!.hp, max: 25 },
      mp: { ...parsed!.mp, max: 16 },
    } as Character;

    const serialized = serializeCharacterData(character);
    expect(serialized.schemaVersion).toBe(1);
    expect(serialized.hp).toEqual({ atual: 17, temp: 4 });
    expect(serialized.mp).toEqual({ atual: 5 });
    expect(serialized).not.toHaveProperty("id");
    expect(serialized).not.toHaveProperty("campanhaId");
    expect(serialized).not.toHaveProperty("dataVersion");
    expect(serialized.hp).not.toHaveProperty("max");
    expect(serialized).toHaveProperty("campaignSpecificLegacyField.custom", true);
  });

  it("rejects unsupported versions and malformed required attributes", () => {
    expect(parseCharacterData({ ...legacyCharacter(), schemaVersion: 9 })).toBeNull();
    expect(parseCharacterData({ ...legacyCharacter(), attributes: { forca: "10" } })).toBeNull();
  });
});



========== ARQUIVO: C:\Users\Ellen\rpg-platform\src\domain\characterData.ts ==========

import { LIMITE_RECURSO } from "../data/dnd";
import type {
  Attributes,
  Character,
  CharacterDataV1,
  InventoryItem,
  Skill,
  Spell,
  Wallet,
} from "../types/character";

const ATTRIBUTE_KEYS: (keyof Attributes)[] = [
  "forca",
  "destreza",
  "constituicao",
  "inteligencia",
  "carisma",
];
const asRecord = (value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null =>
  typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)
    ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)
    : null;

function integer(value: unknown, fallback = 0, min = -999999, max = 999999): number {
  if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value)) return fallback;
  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, Math.trunc(value)));
}

function optionalText(value: unknown): string | undefined {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : undefined;
}

function parseAttributes(value: unknown): Attributes | null {
  const record = asRecord(value);
  if (!record || ATTRIBUTE_KEYS.some((key) => typeof record[key] !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(record[key]))) {
    return null;
  }
  return {
    forca: integer(record.forca, 10, 1, 30),
    destreza: integer(record.destreza, 10, 1, 30),
    constituicao: integer(record.constituicao, 10, 1, 30),
    inteligencia: integer(record.inteligencia, 10, 1, 30),
    carisma: integer(record.carisma, 10, 1, 30),
  };
}

function parseResource(value: unknown): { atual: number; temp?: number } {
  const record = asRecord(value) ?? {};
  const temp = record.temp;
  return {
    atual: integer(record.atual, 0, 0, LIMITE_RECURSO),
    ...(typeof temp === "number" && Number.isFinite(temp)
      ? { temp: integer(temp, 0, 0, LIMITE_RECURSO) }
      : {}),
  };
}

function parseSkills(value: unknown): Skill[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  return value.flatMap((item): Skill[] => {
    const record = asRecord(item);
    if (
      !record ||
      typeof record.id !== "string" ||
      typeof record.nome !== "string" ||
      typeof record.atributo !== "string" ||
      !ATTRIBUTE_KEYS.includes(record.atributo as keyof Attributes)
    ) return [];

    return [{
      id: record.id,
      nome: record.nome,
      atributo: record.atributo as keyof Attributes,
      ...(optionalText(record.descricao) !== undefined ? { descricao: record.descricao as string } : {}),
      treinada: record.treinada === true,
      bonus: integer(record.bonus),
      ...(typeof record.bonusManual === "number" && Number.isFinite(record.bonusManual)
        ? { bonusManual: integer(record.bonusManual) }
        : {}),
    }];
  });
}

function parseBonusAtributos(value: unknown): InventoryItem["bonusAtributos"] {
  const record = asRecord(value);
  if (!record) return undefined;
  const bonus: Partial<Attributes> = {};
  for (const key of ATTRIBUTE_KEYS) {
    if (typeof record[key] === "number" && Number.isFinite(record[key])) {
      bonus[key] = integer(record[key]);
    }
  }
  return bonus;
}

function parseInventory(value: unknown): InventoryItem[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  return value.flatMap((item): InventoryItem[] => {
    const record = asRecord(item);
    if (typeof record?.id !== "string" || typeof record.nome !== "string") return [];
    const bonusAtributos = parseBonusAtributos(record.bonusAtributos);
    const effect = asRecord(record.efeitoMecanico);
    const recurso = effect?.recurso;
    const efeitoMecanico: InventoryItem["efeitoMecanico"] = effect?.tipo === "ATRIBUTO" &&
      typeof effect.atributo === "string" && ATTRIBUTE_KEYS.includes(effect.atributo as keyof Attributes) &&
      typeof effect.valor === "number" && Number.isFinite(effect.valor)
      ? { tipo: "ATRIBUTO", atributo: effect.atributo as keyof Attributes, valor: integer(effect.valor) }
      : effect?.tipo === "RECURSO" &&
          (recurso === "hp" || recurso === "mp") &&
          typeof effect.valor === "number" && Number.isFinite(effect.valor)
        ? { tipo: "RECURSO", recurso, valor: integer(effect.valor) }
        : effect?.tipo === "NENHUM"
          ? { tipo: "NENHUM" }
          : null;
    return [{
      id: record.id,
      nome: record.nome,
      quantidade: integer(record.quantidade, 0, 0, 999999),
      ...(optionalText(record.descricao) !== undefined ? { descricao: record.descricao as string } : {}),
      ...(optionalText(record.imagemUrl) !== undefined ? { imagemUrl: record.imagemUrl as string } : {}),
      ...(typeof record.equipado === "boolean" ? { equipado: record.equipado } : {}),
      ...(bonusAtributos ? { bonusAtributos } : {}),
      ...(typeof record.cargas === "number" || record.cargas === null ? { cargas: record.cargas } : {}),
      ...(typeof record.cargasMaximas === "number" || record.cargasMaximas === null ? { cargasMaximas: record.cargasMaximas } : {}),
      ...(efeitoMecanico ? { efeitoMecanico } : {}),
    }];
  });
}

function parseSpells(value: unknown): Spell[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  return value.flatMap((item): Spell[] => {
    const record = asRecord(item);
    if (typeof record?.id !== "string" || typeof record.nome !== "string") return [];
    return [{
      id: record.id,
      nome: record.nome,
      custo: integer(record.custo, 0, 0, LIMITE_RECURSO),
      descricao: typeof record.descricao === "string" ? record.descricao : "",
    }];
  });
}

function parseWallet(value: unknown): Wallet | undefined {
  const record = asRecord(value);
  if (!record) return undefined;
  return {
    pc: integer(record.pc, 0, 0, 9999999),
    pp: integer(record.pp, 0, 0, 9999999),
    pe: integer(record.pe, 0, 0, 9999999),
    po: integer(record.po, 0, 0, 9999999),
    pl: integer(record.pl, 0, 0, 9999999),
  };
}

/**
 * Decodifica o JSONB de personagens, aceitando o formato legado sem versÃ£o e V1.
 * MÃ¡ximos de PV/PM legados sÃ£o deliberadamente descartados: agora sÃ£o derivados
 * das regras efetivas; valores atuais e temporÃ¡rios permanecem estado persistido.
 */
export function parseCharacterData(value: unknown): CharacterDataV1 | null {
  const record = asRecord(value);
  if (!record || (record.schemaVersion !== undefined && record.schemaVersion !== 1)) {
    return null;
  }

  const attributes = parseAttributes(record.attributes);
  if (!attributes) return null;

  const preserved = { ...record };
  delete preserved.id;
  delete preserved.campanhaId;
  delete preserved.dataVersion;
  delete preserved.xp;

  const character = {
    ...preserved,
    schemaVersion: 1 as const,
    nome: typeof record.nome === "string" ? record.nome : "Aventureiro",
    raca: typeof record.raca === "string" ? record.raca : "Humano",
    classe: typeof record.classe === "string" ? record.classe : "Aventureiro",
    ...(typeof record.classeId === "string" ? { classeId: record.classeId } : {}),
    nivel: integer(record.nivel, 1, 1, 20),
    ...(typeof record.portraitUrl === "string" ? { portraitUrl: record.portraitUrl } : {}),
    ...(typeof record.alinhamento === "string" ? { alinhamento: record.alinhamento } : {}),
    ...(typeof record.qualidades === "string" ? { qualidades: record.qualidades } : {}),
    ...(typeof record.origem === "string" ? { origem: record.origem } : {}),
    ...(typeof record.idade === "string" ? { idade: record.idade } : {}),
    ...(typeof record.historia === "string" ? { historia: record.historia } : {}),
    ...(typeof record.aparencia === "string" ? { aparencia: record.aparencia } : {}),
    ...(typeof record.objetivo === "string" ? { objetivo: record.objetivo } : {}),
    ...(typeof record.defeito === "string" ? { defeito: record.defeito } : {}),
    ...(typeof record.filiacao === "string" ? { filiacao: record.filiacao } : {}),
    ...(typeof record.altura === "number" && Number.isFinite(record.altura) ? { altura: record.altura } : {}),
    ...(typeof record.tracos === "string" ? { tracos: record.tracos } : {}),
    ...(typeof record.defeitos === "string" ? { defeitos: record.defeitos } : {}),
    hp: parseResource(record.hp),
    mp: parseResource(record.mp),
    attributes,
    skills: parseSkills(record.skills),
    inventory: parseInventory(record.inventory),
    spells: parseSpells(record.spells),
    ...(record.carteira && asRecord(record.carteira)
      ? { carteira: parseWallet(record.carteira) }
      : {}),
    notas: typeof record.notas === "string" ? record.notas : "",
  } satisfies CharacterDataV1;

  return character;
}

/** Serializa apenas o contrato persistido, nunca metadados relacionais ou mÃ¡ximos derivados. */
export function serializeCharacterData(character: Character): CharacterDataV1 {
  const persistedFields: Record<string, unknown> = { ...character };
  delete persistedFields.id;
  delete persistedFields.campanhaId;
  delete persistedFields.dataVersion;
  delete persistedFields.xp;
  delete persistedFields.hp;
  delete persistedFields.mp;
  const hp = character.hp;
  const mp = character.mp;

  return {
    ...persistedFields,
    schemaVersion: 1,
    hp: {
      atual: integer(hp.atual, 0, 0, LIMITE_RECURSO),
      ...(hp.temp !== undefined ? { temp: integer(hp.temp, 0, 0, LIMITE_RECURSO) } : {}),
    },
    mp: {
      atual: integer(mp.atual, 0, 0, LIMITE_RECURSO),
      ...(mp.temp !== undefined ? { temp: integer(mp.temp, 0, 0, LIMITE_RECURSO) } : {}),
    },
  } as CharacterDataV1;
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { Character } from "../types/character";
import { applyResourceDelta, setResourceCurrent } from "./characterResources";

const base: Character = {
  id: "resource-test",
  nome: "Nara",
  raca: "Humano",
  classe: "Guerreiro",
  nivel: 1,
  hp: { atual: 9, max: 12, temp: 4 },
  mp: { atual: 5, max: 8, temp: 2 },
  attributes: { forca: 10, destreza: 10, constituicao: 10, inteligencia: 10, carisma: 10 },
  skills: [], inventory: [], spells: [], notas: "",
};

describe("character temporary resources", () => {
  it("uses a temporary reserve before reducing current HP/MP", () => {
    expect(applyResourceDelta(base, "hp", -3, 12)).toMatchObject({ atual: 9, temp: 1 });
    expect(applyResourceDelta(base, "mp", -5, 8)).toMatchObject({ atual: 2, temp: 0 });
  });

  it("does not restore current resources when the maximum grows", () => {
    expect(applyResourceDelta(base, "hp", 0, 30)).toMatchObject({ atual: 9, max: 30, temp: 4 });
  });

  it("clamps only when the maximum decreases and leaves reserve separate", () => {
    expect(setResourceCurrent(base, "hp", base.hp.atual, 6)).toMatchObject({ atual: 6, max: 6, temp: 4 });
  });

  it("restores normal current resource without spending temporary reserve", () => {
    expect(applyResourceDelta(base, "mp", 20, 8)).toMatchObject({ atual: 8, temp: 2 });
  });
});



========== ARQUIVO: C:\Users\Ellen\rpg-platform\src\domain\characterResources.ts ==========

import type { Character } from "../types/character";

export type ResourceKey = "hp" | "mp";

/**
 * Positive deltas restore the normal pool up to its derived maximum. Negative
 * deltas consume the temporary reserve before reducing the normal current value.
 */
export function applyResourceDelta(
  character: Character,
  key: ResourceKey,
  delta: number,
  maximum: number,
): Character[ResourceKey] {
  const resource = character[key];
  const safeMax = Math.max(0, Math.trunc(maximum));
  const safeCurrent = Math.max(0, Math.min(safeMax, Math.trunc(resource.atual)));
  const safeTemporary = Math.max(0, Math.trunc(resource.temp ?? 0));

  if (!Number.isFinite(delta)) {
    return { ...resource, atual: safeCurrent, max: safeMax, temp: safeTemporary };
  }

  const amount = Math.trunc(delta);
  if (amount < 0) {
    const cost = Math.abs(amount);
    const temporaryConsumed = Math.min(safeTemporary, cost);
    const remainingCost = cost - temporaryConsumed;
    return {
      ...resource,
      atual: Math.max(0, safeCurrent - remainingCost),
      max: safeMax,
      temp: safeTemporary - temporaryConsumed,
    };
  }

  return {
    ...resource,
    atual: Math.min(safeMax, safeCurrent + amount),
    max: safeMax,
    temp: safeTemporary,
  };
}

/** Explicit entry in the current-value field does not simulate damage/cost. */
export function setResourceCurrent(
  character: Character,
  key: ResourceKey,
  value: number,
  maximum: number,
): Character[ResourceKey] {
  const safeMax = Math.max(0, Math.trunc(maximum));
  const safeValue = Number.isFinite(value) ? Math.trunc(value) : 0;
  return {
    ...character[key],
    atual: Math.max(0, Math.min(safeMax, safeValue)),
    max: safeMax,
  };
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { Character } from "../types/character";
import { calculateDerivedCharacterStats } from "./characterRules";

function character(overrides: Partial<Character> = {}): Character {
  return {
    id: "character-1",
    nome: "Nara",
    raca: "Humano",
    classe: "Guerreiro",
    classeId: "guerreiro",
    nivel: 1,
    hp: { atual: 10, max: 200 },
    mp: { atual: 10, max: 200 },
    attributes: { forca: 10, destreza: 10, constituicao: 10, inteligencia: 10, carisma: 10 },
    skills: [],
    inventory: [],
    spells: [],
    notas: "",
    ...overrides,
  };
}

const zeroBonus = { forca: 0, destreza: 0, constituicao: 0, inteligencia: 0, carisma: 0 };

describe("calculateDerivedCharacterStats", () => {
  it("keeps character base values unchanged and calculates independent sources once", () => {
    const source = character({
      filiacao: "Sombras Urbanas",
      inventory: [{
        id: "ring",
        nome: "Anel",
        quantidade: 1,
        equipado: true,
        bonusAtributos: { carisma: 2, forca: 1 },
      }],
    });
    const result = calculateDerivedCharacterStats(source);

    expect(result.attributesBase).toEqual(source.attributes);
    expect(result.attributesFinal).toEqual({ forca: 14, destreza: 12, constituicao: 11, inteligencia: 11, carisma: 14 });
    expect(result.breakdown.raca.forca).toBe(1);
    expect(result.breakdown.classe.forca).toBe(2);
    expect(result.breakdown.filiacao.destreza).toBe(1);
    expect(result.breakdown.equipamento.forca).toBe(1);
    expect(source.attributes.forca).toBe(10);
  });

  it("uses the campaign's explicit class override instead of adding it to the global value", () => {
    const result = calculateDerivedCharacterStats(character(), {
      campaignClassBonuses: {
        Guerreiro: {
          atributoBonus: { ...zeroBonus, forca: 4 },
          hpBonus: 3,
          mpBonus: 0,
        },
      },
    });

    expect(result.breakdown.classe.forca).toBe(4);
    expect(result.attributesFinal.forca).toBe(15); // base 10 + human 1 + campaign 4
    expect(result.hpMax).toBe(13); // level-1 class die + campaign PV bonus
  });

  it("resolves a campaign-only class by stable class id", () => {
    const result = calculateDerivedCharacterStats(character({
      classe: "DanÃ§arino das Brumas",
      classeId: "mist-dancer",
    }), {
      classCatalog: [],
      campaignClassContents: [{
        classe_id: "mist-dancer",
        tipo: "CLASSE",
        nome: "DanÃ§arino das Brumas",
        bonus_atributos: { destreza: 3 },
        bonus_hp: 2,
        bonus_mp: 4,
      }],
    });

    expect(result.breakdown.classe.destreza).toBe(3);
    expect(result.hpMax).toBe(10);
    expect(result.mpMax).toBe(23);
  });

  it("ignores unequipped gear and supports supplied relational equipment bonuses", () => {
    const result = calculateDerivedCharacterStats(character({
      classe: "Classe sem bÃ´nus",
      inventory: [{ id: "weapon", nome: "Machado", quantidade: 1, equipado: false, bonusAtributos: { forca: 5 } }],
    }), {
      classCatalog: [],
      equippedItemBonuses: [{ forca: 2 }],
    });

    expect(result.breakdown.equipamento.forca).toBe(2);
    expect(result.attributesFinal.forca).toBe(13); // base 10 + human 1 + supplied gear 2
  });
});



========== ARQUIVO: C:\Users\Ellen\rpg-platform\src\domain\characterRules.ts ==========

import {
  calcularManaMaxima,
  calcularVidaMaxima,
  LIMITE_RECURSO,
} from "../data/dnd";
import {
  AFFILIATION_BONUSES,
  CLASS_BONUSES,
  DEFAULT_CLASS_CATALOG,
  RACE_BONUSES,
  type BaseAttributeKey,
  type CampaignClassBonuses,
  type ClassDefinition,
  type PersonaBonusDefinition,
} from "../data/personaRules";
import type { Attributes, Character, InventoryItem } from "../types/character";

export interface CampaignClassRule {
  classe_id: string;
  tipo: string;
  nome: string;
  bonus_atributos?: Partial<Record<BaseAttributeKey, number>> | null;
  bonus_hp?: number | null;
  bonus_mp?: number | null;
}

export interface CharacterRulesContext {
  classCatalog?: ClassDefinition[];
  campaignClassBonuses?: CampaignClassBonuses;
  campaignClassContents?: CampaignClassRule[];
  equippedItemBonuses?: Partial<Record<BaseAttributeKey, number>>[];
  serverResourceMaximums?: { hpMax: number; mpMax: number };
}

export interface CharacterRuleBreakdown {
  raca: Record<BaseAttributeKey, number>;
  classe: Record<BaseAttributeKey, number>;
  filiacao: Record<BaseAttributeKey, number>;
  equipamento: Record<BaseAttributeKey, number>;
  hp: { raca: number; classe: number; filiacao: number };
  mp: { raca: number; classe: number; filiacao: number };
}

export interface DerivedCharacterStats {
  attributesBase: Attributes;
  attributeBonuses: Record<BaseAttributeKey, number>;
  attributesFinal: Attributes;
  breakdown: CharacterRuleBreakdown;
  hpMax: number;
  mpMax: number;
}

const ATTRIBUTE_KEYS: BaseAttributeKey[] = [
  "forca",
  "destreza",
  "constituicao",
  "inteligencia",
  "carisma",
];
const emptyAttributes = (): Record<BaseAttributeKey, number> => ({
  forca: 0,
  destreza: 0,
  constituicao: 0,
  inteligencia: 0,
  carisma: 0,
});

const normalizeKey = (value: string) =>
  value.normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").trim().toLocaleLowerCase();

function findPersonaBonus(
  catalog: Record<string, PersonaBonusDefinition>,
  key?: string,
): PersonaBonusDefinition | undefined {
  if (!key) return undefined;
  const entry = Object.entries(catalog).find(([name]) => normalizeKey(name) === normalizeKey(key));
  return entry?.[1];
}

function addAttributeBonus(
  target: Record<BaseAttributeKey, number>,
  bonus?: Partial<Record<BaseAttributeKey, number>> | null,
): void {
  if (!bonus) return;
  for (const attribute of ATTRIBUTE_KEYS) {
    const value = bonus[attribute];
    if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
      target[attribute] += Math.trunc(value);
    }
  }
}

function getClassDefinition(
  character: Pick<Character, "classe" | "classeId">,
  catalog: ClassDefinition[],
): ClassDefinition | undefined {
  return catalog.find((definition) =>
    (character.classeId && definition.id === character.classeId) ||
    normalizeKey(definition.nome) === normalizeKey(character.classe) ||
    definition.aliases.some((alias) => normalizeKey(alias) === normalizeKey(character.classe)),
  );
}

function getCampaignOverride(
  character: Pick<Character, "classe" | "classeId">,
  overrides?: CampaignClassBonuses,
) {
  if (!overrides) return undefined;
  const entry = Object.entries(overrides).find(([key]) =>
    normalizeKey(key) === normalizeKey(character.classe) ||
    normalizeKey(key) === normalizeKey(character.classeId ?? ""),
  );
  return entry?.[1];
}

function getCampaignClassContent(
  character: Pick<Character, "classe" | "classeId">,
  contents: CampaignClassRule[] = [],
) {
  return contents.find((content) =>
    content.tipo === "CLASSE" && (
      (character.classeId && content.classe_id === character.classeId) ||
      normalizeKey(content.nome) === normalizeKey(character.classe)
    ),
  );
}

function getEquipmentBonuses(
  character: Pick<Character, "inventory">,
  context: CharacterRulesContext,
): Partial<Record<BaseAttributeKey, number>>[] {
  if (context.equippedItemBonuses) return context.equippedItemBonuses;
  return (character.inventory ?? [])
    .filter((item: InventoryItem) => item.equipado === true)
    .map((item) => item.bonusAtributos ?? {});
}

/** Calcula os totais sem modificar nem persistir atributos-base ou mÃ¡ximos derivados. */
export function calculateDerivedCharacterStats(
  character: Pick<Character, "raca" | "classe" | "classeId" | "filiacao" | "nivel" | "attributes" | "inventory">,
  context: CharacterRulesContext = {},
): DerivedCharacterStats {
  const catalog = context.classCatalog ?? DEFAULT_CLASS_CATALOG;
  const classDefinition = getClassDefinition(character, catalog);
  const override = getCampaignOverride(character, context.campaignClassBonuses);
  const campaignClass = getCampaignClassContent(character, context.campaignClassContents);
  const raceBonus = findPersonaBonus(RACE_BONUSES, character.raca);
  const affiliationBonus = findPersonaBonus(AFFILIATION_BONUSES, character.filiacao);
  const defaultClassBonus = findPersonaBonus(CLASS_BONUSES, character.classe);

  // PrecedÃªncia: override explÃ­cito da campanha > classe criada na campanha > catÃ¡logo > regras locais.
  const classAttributeBonus = override?.atributoBonus
    ?? (campaignClass
      ? campaignClass.bonus_atributos
      : classDefinition?.atributoBonus ?? defaultClassBonus?.atributoBonus);
  const classHpBonus = override?.hpBonus
    ?? (campaignClass
      ? campaignClass.bonus_hp
      : classDefinition?.hpBonus ?? defaultClassBonus?.hpBonus);
  const classMpBonus = override?.mpBonus
    ?? (campaignClass
      ? campaignClass.bonus_mp
      : classDefinition?.mpBonus ?? defaultClassBonus?.mpBonus);

  const breakdown: CharacterRuleBreakdown = {
    raca: emptyAttributes(),
    classe: emptyAttributes(),
    filiacao: emptyAttributes(),
    equipamento: emptyAttributes(),
    hp: {
      raca: raceBonus?.hpBonus ?? 0,
      classe: classHpBonus ?? 0,
      filiacao: affiliationBonus?.hpBonus ?? 0,
    },
    mp: {
      raca: raceBonus?.mpBonus ?? 0,
      classe: classMpBonus ?? 0,
      filiacao: affiliationBonus?.mpBonus ?? 0,
    },
  };

  addAttributeBonus(breakdown.raca, raceBonus?.atributoBonus);
  addAttributeBonus(breakdown.classe, classAttributeBonus);
  addAttributeBonus(breakdown.filiacao, affiliationBonus?.atributoBonus);
  for (const equipmentBonus of getEquipmentBonuses(character, context)) {
    addAttributeBonus(breakdown.equipamento, equipmentBonus);
  }

  const attributeBonuses = emptyAttributes();
  const attributesFinal = {} as Attributes;
  for (const attribute of ATTRIBUTE_KEYS) {
    attributeBonuses[attribute] =
      breakdown.raca[attribute] +
      breakdown.classe[attribute] +
      breakdown.filiacao[attribute] +
      breakdown.equipamento[attribute];
    attributesFinal[attribute] = character.attributes[attribute] + attributeBonuses[attribute];
  }

  const level = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(character.nivel || 1));
  const hpMax = Math.max(1, Math.min(
    LIMITE_RECURSO,
    calcularVidaMaxima(character.classe, level, attributesFinal.constituicao) +
      breakdown.hp.raca + breakdown.hp.classe + breakdown.hp.filiacao,
  ));
  const mpMax = Math.max(0, Math.min(
    LIMITE_RECURSO,
    calcularManaMaxima(attributesFinal.inteligencia) +
      breakdown.mp.raca + breakdown.mp.classe + breakdown.mp.filiacao,
  ));

  return {
    attributesBase: { ...character.attributes },
    attributeBonuses,
    attributesFinal,
    breakdown,
    hpMax: context.serverResourceMaximums?.hpMax ?? hpMax,
    mpMax: context.serverResourceMaximums?.mpMax ?? mpMax,
  };
}

import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";
import { beforeEach, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

function renderCampaign() {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={["/campanha/campaign-1"]}>
      <Routes>

    });

      renderCampaign();

      fireEvent.change(await screen.findByLabelText("Nome"), {
        target: { value: "Passo das sombras" },
      });
      fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("DescriÃ§Ã£o"), {
        target: { value: "Move-se sem ser percebido." },
      });
      fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("NÃ­vel de desbloqueio"), {

      await waitFor(() => {

    vi.mocked(listarPersonagensDaCampanha).mockResolvedValueOnce([{
      id: "character-1",
      campanhaId: "campaign-1",
      nome: "Lira",

      { itemId: "item-a", nome: "PoÃ§Ã£o rubra" },
      { itemId: "item-b", nome: "PoÃ§Ã£o azul" },
    ] as never);
    vi.mocked(entregarItemCampanha)
      .mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("Falha temporÃ¡ria"))
      .mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("Falha temporÃ¡ria"))
      .mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("Falha temporÃ¡ria"))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(1);

    renderCampaign();
    fireEvent.change(await screen.findByLabelText("Tipo de recompensa"), {
      target: { value: "ITEM" },
    });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("Item"), { target: { value: "item-a" } });
    const form = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Entregar Recompensa" }).closest("form");
    expect(form).not.toBeNull();

    fireEvent.submit(form!);
    await screen.findByText("Falha temporÃ¡ria");
    const firstRequestId = vi.mocked(entregarItemCampanha).mock.calls[0][4];

    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("Item"), { target: { value: "item-b" } });
    fireEvent.submit(form!);
    await waitFor(() => expect(entregarItemCampanha).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2));
    await screen.findByText("Falha temporÃ¡ria");
    const secondRequestId = vi.mocked(entregarItemCampanha).mock.calls[1][4];
    expect(secondRequestId).not.toBe(firstRequestId);

    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("Item"), { target: { value: "item-a" } });
    fireEvent.submit(form!);
    await waitFor(() => expect(entregarItemCampanha).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3));
    const retriedFirstItemId = vi.mocked(entregarItemCampanha).mock.calls[2][4];
    expect(retriedFirstItemId).toBe(firstRequestId);
    await screen.findByText("Falha temporÃ¡ria");

    fireEvent.submit(form!);
    await waitFor(() => expect(entregarItemCampanha).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(4));
    expect(vi.mocked(entregarItemCampanha).mock.calls[3][4]).toBe(firstRequestId);
    expect(await screen.findByText("ConcluÃ­do! 50 PoÃ§Ã£o rubra foram entregues para 1 personagens do grupo.")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("confirma a cÃ³pia do convite apenas apÃ³s sucesso e oferece cÃ³pia manual na falha", async () => {
    const originalClipboard = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(navigator, "clipboard");
    const writeText = vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error("Bloqueada pelo navegador"));
    Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", {
      configurable: true,
      value: { writeText },
    });

    try {
      renderCampaign();
      fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Copiar" }));

      expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
        "NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel copiar o convite. Selecione o cÃ³digo para copiÃ¡-lo manualmente.",
      );
      expect(screen.getAllByText("ABC123")).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(screen.queryByText("Convite copiado!")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
      expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith("ABC123");
    } finally {
      if (originalClipboard) {
        Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", originalClipboard);
      } else {
        Reflect.deleteProperty(navigator, "clipboard");
      }
    }
  });

  it("confirma que o cÃ³digo foi copiado quando a Clipboard API resolve", async () => {
    const originalClipboard = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(navigator, "clipboard");
    const writeText = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", {
      configurable: true,
      value: { writeText },
    });

    try {
      renderCampaign();
      fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Copiar" }));

      expect(await screen.findByText("Convite copiado!")).toBeInTheDocument();
      expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Copiado!" })).toBeInTheDocument();
      expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith("ABC123");
    } finally {
      if (originalClipboard) {
        Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", originalClipboard);
      } else {
        Reflect.deleteProperty(navigator, "clipboard");
      }
    }
  });

  it("nÃ£o declara sucesso se a Clipboard API estiver indisponÃ­vel", async () => {
    const originalClipboard = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(navigator, "clipboard");
    Reflect.deleteProperty(navigator, "clipboard");

    try {
      renderCampaign();
      fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Copiar" }));

      expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent("copiÃ¡-lo manualmente");
      expect(screen.getAllByText("ABC123")).toHaveLength(2);
    } finally {
      if (originalClipboard) {
        Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", originalClipboard);
      }
    }
  });

  it("pede confirmaÃ§Ã£o antes de chamar a exclusÃ£o", async () => {
    renderCampaign();

    const dangerZone = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Zona de perigo" });
    expect(dangerZone).toHaveTextContent("A exclusÃ£o remove a campanha e nÃ£o pode ser desfeita.");
    fireEvent.click(within(dangerZone).getByRole("button", { name: "Excluir campanha" }));

    expect(screen.getByRole("alertdialog")).toHaveTextContent("Excluir â€œAventura de Testeâ€?");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Confirmar exclusÃ£o" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(excluirCampanha).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Cancelar" }));
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alertdialog")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(excluirCampanha).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("exclui a campanha e retorna Ã  lista apÃ³s confirmaÃ§Ã£o", async () => {
    renderCampaign();

    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Excluir campanha" }));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Confirmar exclusÃ£o" }));

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(excluirCampanha).toHaveBeenCalledWith("campaign-1");
    });
    expect(await screen.findByText("Lista de campanhas")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("nÃ£o mostra a opÃ§Ã£o para jogadores", async () => {
    vi.mocked(getCampaignAccess).mockResolvedValueOnce({ isMaster: false, startingGold: 0 });
    renderCampaign();

    expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "Meu personagem nesta aventura" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Excluir campanha" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

function criarIdClasseCampanha() {
  return `camp-${globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID?.() ?? Date.now()}`;
}

function criarRequestId(): string {
  if (globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID) return globalThis.crypto.randomUUID();
  return "xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx".replace(/[xy]/g, (token) => {
    const value = Math.floor(Math.random() * 16);
    return (token === "x" ? value : (value & 0x3) | 0x8).toString(16);
  });
}
